# PD2 Resurrected native research inventory

Source: [830365199838](https://github.com/synpoox/pd2-resurrected/tree/8303651998382477fdac695d7dbb2cf11ce311df). See the [intake and provenance](pd2-resurrected-intake.md).

Generated by `python scripts/reverse-engineering/pd2-native-intake.py`; the [JSON catalogue](pd2-resurrected-native-inventory.json) retains excerpts, contracts, source hashes and line ranges.

**Imported source claims only.** PD2 x86 addresses, D2R x64 addresses, Core providers, SDK services and plugin helpers must remain distinct. Registry overlap and byte equality do not establish semantic identity, ABI or runtime qualification.

Scanned 269 files and retained 4604 records. There are 4 distinct PD2-owned named address values, 256 distinct source Engine call symbols, and 255 of those lack an available PD2-owned literal address.

**Unavailable dependency:** PD2 pins d2rAI-toolkit `14a5433dfe82d98a1966c247f9f123e62cc3230c`. Its declared repository returned 404; that exact revision was unavailable in the checked fork/local checkout. Toolkit-only declarations are unresolved; no replacement revision was used.

Search: `python scripts/reverse-engineering/pd2-native-intake.py --query SocketFillerMods` or `--query Tables --kind typed-field-access`. Queries need only the versioned JSON.
